Let's think about algorithm to improve a given sequence:
We want minimize max c. Could use subgradient of p-norm. Let's derive efficient update:
Given a (length n), c = linear convolution a*a length 2n-1.
Use weights w = c^p (or c^{p-1})? Sum objective J = sum c^p. Gradient dJ/da_j = 2p sum_k c_k^{p-1} a_{k-j}. Let G_j = sum_k c_k^{p-1} a_{k-j}. Scale doesn't matter.
Projected gradient step on simplex:
a_temp = a - step * G (since gradient direction). Then project to simplex (sum=1, >=0). But G may have large magnitude. Adaptive step via line search minimizing J.
Alternatively use multiplicative: a_temp = a / (eps + G), normalize. This ensures positivity but can blow if G small. Damping: a_new = (1-d)*a + d*normalize(a/G). 
Which is better? Multiplicative like natural gradient on simplex; often good for nonneg.

Let's test conceptually on uniform n large: c triangular. p maybe 8. weights w=c^7 peaked center. G_j = convolution of a and w_rev coefficient. Since a uniform, G_j = average of sliding window of w of length n. w is centered peaked; sliding sum is maximal at center, smaller at edges. So a/G largest at edges, normalizing yields U-shaped sequence. That likely reduces central convolution but may create edge peaks c_0 and c_{2n-2} ~a_0^2. If edges get too large, weights at edges grow and next iterations reduce edges. It may find balanced shape with no single peak. This is reminiscent of finding "flat convolution" via multiplicative weight.

def multiplicative_update(a, c, p, K, damp):
   n=len(a); cmax=max(c); if cmax<=0 return a
   # weights for top K maybe:
   if K >= len(c): idx = np.arange(len(c)); w = (c/cmax)**p
   else: idx = top K; w = (c[idx]/cmax)**p
   wsum = w.sum(); w/=wsum
   G=zeros(n)
   for k, weight in zip(idx, w):
       start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(n,k+1)
       i_low=k-(end-1); i_high=k-start
       G[start:end] += weight * a[i_low:i_high+1][::-1]
   # G may be zero; scale by mean positive
   gmean = G.mean() maybe zero? Since a,c positive, G positive if all weights positive. But top K weights positive, G_j might be zero if no weight overlaps? Use floor.
   ratio = G / max(gmean, eps)
   new = a / (eps + ratio) ??? If ratio high, reduce. But if ratio mean=1, new roughly a. Need normalize. Use sign: If G high, reduce, so a/(G/gmean) works. For uniform, G center high ratio>1 => center reduced, edges ratio<1 => increased. Good.

   But if some G very small, new huge; normalize brings down? Could cause instability. Alternative: new = a * (gmean / (G + floor*gmean)). Or use centered log: a_new = a * exp(-alpha*(G-gmean)/gmean). But needs positivity.

   Let's use: Gnorm = G / np.maximum(G.min? no). Use shifted: denom = G + lambda * G.mean(). Then a_new = a / denom, normalize; damping: a_new = (1-d)*a + d*a_new. Detailed algorithm optimization:
```
def optimize_sequence(a, time_limit, rng, exact_checker):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    n = len(a)
    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
    base_score = 2*n*c.max()
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = base_score
    best_exact = None
    exact_every = 50
    stall = 0
    p = 4.0
    K = choose_K(n)
    etas = [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]
    last_eta = 0.25
    while remaining > 2s:
       # adaptive K/p based on score/stall
       G = gradient_top(a,c,K,p)
       u = center_and_scale(G)
       accepted=False
       # generate etas around last_eta
       for eta in [last_eta*0.5,last_eta,last_eta*2] + [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2]:
           if eta<=0: continue
           z = -eta*u
           z = clip(z,-15,15)
           new = a * exp(z); s=new.sum(); if s<=0 continue; new /= s
           # Optional sym candidate
           for cand in (new, 0.5*(new+new[::-1])):
               cand=normalize_array(cand); if not cand continue
               sc = self_score_fft(cand)
               if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_score = sc; best_a = cand.copy(); a = cand; c = self_convolution_fft(cand); accepted=True; last_eta=eta; stall=0; break
           if accepted: break
       if not accepted:
           # random perturbation
           img = random_perturb(a, rng, n)
           sc = self_score_fft(img)
           if sc < best_score: accept
           else:
              stall +=1
              # change hyperparams
              if stall % 20 == 0: p *=1.2? K? etas smaller; maybe sym
       # exact evaluate if best_score improved and time
       if best_score < best_exact_score - 1e-6 and iterations % exact_every ==0:
           es=evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()); if finite update best_exact
    return best_a,best_score,best_exact
```

   n=len(a); L=2*n-2; wr=w[::-1]

   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); W=np.fft.rfft(wr,N); conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:L+1]; return conv[::-1]

```
def project_simplex(v):
    n = len(v)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1))[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```
This returns sum=1 if v arbitrary? Yes for projection onto sum=1 simplex.

def self_convolution_fft(a):
    n = a.size
    nfft=1
    need=2*n-1
    while nfft<need: nfft <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:need]
    # tiny negatives
    return c
```

Line search/update using exponentiated gradient:
```
def exponentiated_candidate(a, G, eta):
    u = G - G.mean()
    scale = np.max(np.abs(u))
    if scale < eps: return None
    z = -eta * u / scale
    z = np.clip(z, -20, 20)
    cand = a * np.exp(z)
    s = cand.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: return None
    cand /= s
    cand = np.clip(cand,0,1000)
    cand /= cand.sum()
    return cand
```
For eta maybe >1. Use scale max abs so step size dimensionless. Good.

Let's write helper `normalize_array`:
```
def normalize_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size ==0: return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
       # fallback uniform
       return np.full(a.shape, 1.0/a.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    a /= s
    # if max >1000? sum1 prevents
    return a
```
But if array has large values? Clip before sum? If clipping reduces sum maybe okay. We usually sum1.

```

Line search strategy:
```
eta = 0.2
stall = 0
for iter:
  G=grad
  u=center_scale
  accepted=False
  for try_idx, factor in [(0,eta),(1,eta*0.5),(2,eta*0.25),(3,eta*0.1)]:
     cand=exp_update(a,u,scale,factor)
     c_new=conv(cand)
     score_new=...
     p_new=...
     if score_new < best_score - 1e-12:
         accept as best and current; eta=factor*1.1 maybe; break
     elif score_new <= best_score + 1e-9 and p_new < current_p - 1e-15:

  if not accepted:
     # try random or reduce p/eta
```

Pseudo optimize:
```
def optimize_from_start(a0, time_budget, rng):
    a = normalize_array(a0)
    if a is None: return ...
    s = self_convolution_fft(a); score=score_from_conv(a,c); pnorm = sum(c**p?)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=0.2
    beta=10
    stall=0
    iter=0
    phase_end = time.monotonic()+ time_budget
    while time.monotonic() < phase_end and score > 1.45:
        cmax = max(c)
        weights = exp_weights(c,cmax,beta)
        G = correlation_aw_fft(a, weights)
        u = G - G.mean(); scale=max(abs(u).max(),1e-12)
        accepted=False
        # try eta, eta/2, eta/4, maybe eta*2
        for trial in [0,1,2,3]:
            e = eta * (0.5**trial)
            if e <=0: continue
            cand = exp_update(a,u,scale,e)
            if cand is None: continue
            c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
            sc2 = 2*n*max(c2)/(cand.sum()**2)
            p2 = smooth_value(c2,beta)  # maybe sum exp(beta(r-1))
            if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                # accept and update best
                best_a=cand; best_score=sc2; a=cand; c=c2; score=sc2; accepted=True; eta=min(2.0,e*1.25?); stall=0; break
            elif sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and p2 < best_psmooth? or current_psmooth:
                # accept current (maybe not best)
                a=cand; c=c2; score=sc2; if sc2 < best_score: update; accepted=True; eta=e; break
        if not accepted:
            # random/perturb
            cand = random_perturb(a,rng, ...)
            c2=conv(cand); sc2=...
            if sc2 < best_score + 1e-7:
                 a=cand; c=c2; score=sc2; if sc2<best_score update; accepted=True
            if not accepted:
                 # try reverse blend
                 cand = normalize(0.5*(a+reverse))
                 ...
            if not accepted:
                 # adjust hyperparams, reduce beta/eta
                 eta = max(0.02, eta*0.5); beta = max(2, beta*0.7)
                 stall +=1
                 if stall > 50:
                     # restart from best with random shape blend
                     a = normalize(0.5*best_a + 0.5*power_shape(n))
                     c=conv(a); score=score
                     eta=0.1; beta=10
        iter +=1
        if iter % 25 ==0: maybe exact eval best
    return best
```

Let's write helper `get_initial_global()`:
```
def get_initial_global():
    seq = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if seq is None: return None
    try:
       arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
       arr = arr.ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if arr.size ==0: return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    return arr/s
```

Power shapes:
```
def power_alpha_shape(n, alpha):
    i = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = i**(-alpha)
    # maybe add a small term to avoid too sharp? no
    return normalize_array(a)
```
Central binomial:
```
def central_binomial_shape(n):
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    return normalize_array(a)
```

Blend shapes:
```
def blend_normalize(arr1, arr2, mix):
    cand = (1-mix)*arr1 + mix*arr2
    return normalize_array(cand)
```

```
def generated_candidates(n, rng):
   cands=[]
   b=central_binomial_shape(n)
   cands.append(('b', b, 1.0))
   for gamma in np.linspace(0,1.5,8):
       i=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       a=b*(1+gamma*i)

Let's define `smooth_value(c,beta)` with logsumexp stable:
```
cm = max(c); z = beta*(c-cm); z = clip(z, -700, 700); logsum = np.max(z)+log(np.sum(exp(z-maxz))); return cm + logsum/beta
```
Since sum positive. If beta large and only one active, F≈cm.

for e in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25]:

Let's define robust optimize:
```
def optimize_sequence(start, time_budget, rng):
   a=normalize_array(start)
   if a is None: a=central_binomial_shape(256)
   n=len(a)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=inf; best_exact=inf
   c=conv(a); score=score_from_conv(a,c)
   best_score=score; best_exact maybe evaluate once? maybe not inside start.
   phases...
   cur=a.copy(); cur_c=c; cur_score=score
   iter=0; exact_counter=0
   for beta,frac in phases:
      phase_end = t_start + phase_deadline
      eta = 0.15 / sqrt(max(beta,1)/10.0)
      F = smooth_value(cur_c,beta)
      stall=0
      while time.monotonic() < phase_end and cur_score > 1.45? (no, continue)
         weights=soft_weights(cur_c,beta)
         G=corr(cur,weights)
         u=center_scale
         accepted=False
         for e in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.125]:
             cand=exp_update
             c2=conv(cand)
             sc2=score
             F2=smooth_value(c2,beta)
             if sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and F2 < F - 1e-18:
                 # accept
                 cur=cand; cur_c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=F2; eta = e*1.1 if accepted at first else e; stall=0; accepted=True
                 if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score=sc2; best_a=cand.copy()
                    exact_counter=0? 
                 break
         if not accepted:
             # try random or restore
             stall +=1
             if stall % 10 ==0:
                # random perturb current
                cand=random_perturb(cur, rng, 0.05)

def generate_candidates(n, rng):
    cands=[]
    b=central_binomial_shape(n)
    cands.append(b)
    rev=b[::-1]
    for gamma in np.linspace(0.0,0.6,7): # small tail boost
       a=b*(1.0 + gamma*(np.arange(n)/(n-1 if n>1 else 1)))
       cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    for mix in np.linspace(0.0,0.25,3): # small reverse blend (not >0.25 to avoid central blow)
       a=(1.0-mix)*b + mix*rev
       cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    for alpha in [0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6,0.4]:
       cands.append(power_alpha_shape(n,alpha))
    # offset r?
    for r in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:
       i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
       a=(i+1.0 + r*n)**(-0.5)
       cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    # random smooth
    sigmas=[0.2,0.5]
    for sig in sigmas:
       a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sig,n)); cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    return cands
```

```
def optimize_sequence(start, time_budget, rng):
    start = np.asarray(start, dtype=np.float64)
    a = normalize_array(start)
    if a is None:
        a = uniform_array
        return a,2.0
    n = a.size
    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
    cur_score = score_from_conv(a,c)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score=2.0; best_a=...
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + max(2.0, float(time_budget))
    phases = ((5,0.15),(10,0.20),(20,0.25),(40,0.25),(80,0.15))
    # If time_budget small, use less phases? Use all scaled.
    phase_start=t0
    stall_reset_counter=0
    last_exact? none
    for beta,frac in phases:
        phase_end = min(end, phase_start + frac*float(time_budget))
        phase_start = phase_end
        if time.monotonic() > phase_end - 0.2: continue
        # If current best score <= best_score? current maybe.
        eta = 0.2 / np.sqrt(max(1.0, beta/10.0))
        F = smooth_max_value(c, beta)
        stall=0
        phase_iter=0
        while time.monotonic() < phase_end - 0.05:
            # If current drifted too far from best
            if cur_score > best_score + 0.01:
                a = best_a.copy(); c = self_convolution_fft(a); cur_score=best_score; F=smooth_max_value(c,beta); eta=0.05; stall=0
            # compute G
            w = softmax_weights(c,beta)
            G = correlation_aw_fft(a,w)
            u = G - np.mean(G)
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(u))) if u.size else 0.0
            if scale < 1e-14:
                # no gradient; random
                cand = block_perturb(a, rng, 0.1)
                if cand is not None:
                   c2=... sc2=...
                   if sc2 < best_score: update
                stall +=1
                eta *=1.5
                continue
            accepted=False
            # candidate list: exp eta, exp eta/2, ratio alpha=1 damp=0.5, proj eta?
            # To limit, generate in loop
            for mode, param in (('exp', eta), ('exp', eta*0.5), ('ratio', 1.0), ('proj', eta)):
                if mode=='exp':
                   cand=exponentiated_candidate(a,u,scale,param)
                elif mode=='ratio':
                   cand=ratio_candidate(a,G,param) # param alpha
                else:
                   cand=projected_candidate(a,u,scale,param)
                if cand is None: continue
                c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
                sc2 = score_from_conv(cand,c2)
                F2 = smooth_max_value(c2,beta)
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    # accept best
                    best_score=sc2; best_a=cand.copy()
                    a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=F2
                    eta = param if mode=='exp' or mode=='proj' else eta
                    accepted=True; stall=0; break
                if sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and F2 < F - 1e-18:
                    a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=F2
                    if mode=='exp' or mode=='proj': eta = max(0.01, param*1.05)
                    else: eta = max(0.01, eta*0.9)
                    accepted=True; stall=0; break
            if not accepted:
                stall +=1
                eta = max(0.01, eta*0.6)
                if stall % 7 ==0:
                    # try random perturb of current
                    sigma = 0.03 + 0.02*(stall%5)
                    cand = block_perturb(a, rng, sigma)
                    if cand is None: cand = random_noise_perturb(a,rng,sigma)
                    if cand is not None:
                       c2=...; sc2=...
                       if sc2 < best_score:
                           best_score=sc2; best_a=cand.copy(); a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=smooth...
                       elif sc2 <= best_score + 0.005:
                           # accept as current even if not best?
                           a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=smooth...
                if stall > 80:
                    # restore best, maybe new random from best
                    a = best_a.copy(); c = self_convolution_fft(a); cur_score=best_score; F=smooth...
                    cell = block_perturb(a, rng, 0.1)
                    if accepted? no
            phase_iter +=1
    return best_a, best_score
```

`projected_candidate`: Need `project_simplex`.
```
def project_simplex(v):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   n=v.size
   u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   cum=np.cumsum(u)
   rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)) > (cum - 1.0))[0]
   if rho.size==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   r=rho[-1]
   theta=(cum[r]-1.0)/(r+1.0)
   w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
   s=w.sum(); if s<... fallback
   return w/s
```
This projection is for simplex sum=1. If v has inf, no.
Candidate: `v = a - eta*(u/scale)`.

def exponentiated_candidate(a,u,scale,eta):

    new = a * np.exp(z)
    return normalize_array(new)

def resample_sequence(arr, target):
    if arr.size <= target: return arr
    n=arr.size; out=np.zeros(target)
    for i in range(target):
       start=int(i*n/target); end=max(start+1,int((i+1)*n/target))
       out[i]=arr[start:end].mean()
    return normalize_array(out)
```

Let's think about numerical details in FFT functions.

`self_convolution_fft(a)`:
```
def self_convolution_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.zeros(0)
    need = 2*n - 1
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < need:
        nfft <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=nfft)
    c = c[:need]
    # clean tiny negatives
    # c[c < 0] = 0? For max and weights, negative small can affect? Use np.maximum(c, 0) for weights/score? Score max should be max(c,0). But exact max positive. Use np.where(c>0,c,0) maybe.
    return c
```
Do not hard clip all negatives? If some negative due roundoff, okay. But for max, if all negative impossible. We can return raw; max positive. For weights, clip.

`correlation_aw_fft(a,w)`:
```
def correlation_aw_fft(a,w):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); w=np.asarray(w,float)
    n=a.size; m=w.size
    need=n+m-1
    nfft=1
    while nfft < need: nfft <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],nfft)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,nfft)
    L=m-1 # 2n-2
    if L < 0: return np.zeros(n)
    start = n-1
    stop = L+1
    if start > conv.size: return np.zeros(n)
    seg = conv[start:stop]
    G = seg[::-1]
    # ensure length n; if stop clipped? nfft >= need so stop <= conv.size.
    return G
```
For n=1, m=1, need=1, L=0, start=0, stop=1, seg [conv0], reversed length1. Good.
Need `w[::-1]` creates view? stride -1, FFT handles? np.fft may make copy. Fine. Could use `np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])` for speed.
```
wr = np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])
```
Memory O(2n). Good.

`softmax_weights(c,beta)`:
```
def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, float)
    cm = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cm <= 1e-300 or beta <=0:
        return np.ones_like(c)
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    # For performance, clip lower -700
    z = np.where(z < -700.0, -700.0, z)
    w = np.exp(z)
    # Ensure at least max weights 1
    if np.max(w) <=0: w = np.ones_like(c)
    return w
```
If c has negative large? cm positive. Good.

`smooth_max_value(c,beta)`:
```
def smooth_max_value(c,beta):
   cm=max; if cm<=0 return 0
   z=beta*(c-cm)
   zmax=float(np.max(z))
   # zmax should 0
   s=np.sum(np.exp(z - zmax))
   if s<=0: return cm
   return cm + (zmax + np.log(s))/beta
```
For beta large, z-zmax <=0. Good.

`score_from_conv(a,c)`:
```
def score_from_conv(a,c):
   n=a.size
   s=float(np.sum(a))
   if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf
   m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   if m < 0: m=0.0
   return float(2.0 * n * m / (s*s))
```

`normalize_array`: ensure finite. If input is list, ok.
```
def normalize_array(a):
   try: arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   except Exception: return None
   if arr.size==0: return None
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12:
      arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
      s = arr.size
   arr = arr / s
   # If any >1000 after normalization? sum1 so no.
   return arr
```
If arr has huge values, clipping before sum may reduce sum; ok.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(arr, target):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
   if arr.size <= target: return normalize_array(arr)
   n=arr.size
   out=np.zeros(target,float)
   for i in range(target):
      a=int(i*n/target); b=int((i+1)*n/target)
      if b<=a: b=a+1
      out[i]=arr[a:b].mean()
   return normalize_array(out)
```
Loop target up to 12000, if arr huge maybe slow but okay; could vectorize with cumulative but fine. If arr size enormous (1e6), slicing mean in Python loop 12000 okay. Good.

`get_initial_global` as above.

Shapes:
```
def central_binomial_shape(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return np.zeros(0)
    a=np.empty(n,float)
    a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
       a[i]=a[i-1]*(2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i)
    return normalize_array(a)
```
For n large loop 10000 okay. Could vectorize with recurrence? loop fine.

`power_alpha_shape(n, alpha)`:
```
i=np.arange(1,n+1,float)
a=np.power(i, -float(alpha))
return normalize_array(a)
```

`generate_candidates(n, rng)`: ensure no lambdas. Use list. For n=1 handle ratio arange. Let's write:
```
def generate_candidates(n, rng):
    cands=[]
    n=int(n)
    if n < 2:
       arr=np.ones(2); return [normalize_array(arr)]
    b=central_binomial_shape(n)
    cands.append(b)
    pos=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1: pos_scale = pos/(n-1)
    else: pos_scale=zeros
    for gamma in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
       a = b * (1.0 + gamma*pos_scale)
       cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    rev=b[::-1]
    for mix in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
       a = (1.0-mix)*b + mix*rev
       cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    for alpha in (0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6,0.4,0.65):
       cands.append(power_alpha_shape(n, alpha))
    for r in (0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0):
       a = np.power(pos + 1.0 + r*float(n), -0.5)
       cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    # random
    for sigma in (0.2,0.5):
       a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
       cands.append(normalize_array(a))
    # random block smooth? no
    filtered=[]
    for arr in cands:
       arr=normalize_array(arr)
       if arr is not None and arr.size==n:
          filtered.append(arr)
    return filtered
```
Number: 1+5+3+6+4+2=21. Good.

But small reverse mix 0.2 could still create central cross, but scan may reject. Since we only select top by FFT, if worse not optimized. Good.

Could add shape with alternating attenuation to reduce central cross while boosting tail? Example `b * (0.5+0.5*cos(pi*i/(n-1)))` (peaks left, low right?) no. Or `b * (1 + gamma * cos(2*pi*i/n))` nonnegative if gamma<1. High/low alternating may reduce central. Include a couple:

for amp in (0.2,0.4):

```

Need `ratio_candidate` uses G; if G all zeros, return None.
```
def ratio_candidate(a, G, alpha=1.0):
    Gp=np.maximum(G,0.0)
    gm=float(np.mean(Gp))
    if gm <= 1e-300:
       return None
    denom = Gp + alpha*gm + 1e-300
    new = a / denom
    s=float(np.sum(new))
    if s <= 1e-300: return None
    new /= s
    cand = 0.5*a + 0.5*new
    return normalize_array(cand)
```
Could have multiple alpha; just one.

`project_simplex` details: Need handle n=1. Standard:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
       v=np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    inds=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u * inds) > (cssv - 1.0)
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if rho.size ==0:
       w=np.full(n,1.0/n)
    else:
       r=rho[-1]
       theta=(cssv[r]-1.0)/(r+1.0)
       w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return w/s
```
This is standard but note condition for projection onto sum=1: find largest rho such that u_rho - (cssv_rho -1)/rho >0. Equivalent `u * rho > cssv -1`. Good.

Candidate `v = a - param * (u/scale)`. Since u center, projection. `param` maybe eta. Good.

Random perturbation helpers:
```
def random_noise_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return None
    noise=rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), a.size)
    cand=a*np.exp(noise)
    return normalize_array(cand)

def block_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n<2: return random_noise_perturb(a,rng,sigma)
    minw=max(1,n//500)
    maxw=max(minw+1,n//10)
    width=int(rng.integers(minw, maxw))
    start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
    cand=a.copy()
    cand[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=1)))
    # maybe mirror with small probability? use if rng.random()<0.2: cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
    return normalize_array(cand)
```
`rng.integers(high, low?)` actual `rng.integers(minw, maxw)` high exclusive. If maxw <= minw, set maxw=minw+1. Need handle n-width+1 positive.
```
max_start = n - width
if max_start <=0: start=0 else start=int(rng.integers(0,max_start+1))
```
Good.

In optimize, when random accepted as current, need recompute F (same beta). If random score < best, update best. If random slightly worse but <=best+0.005, accept current to explore; but then current F maybe not lower. This could allow drift. Use only if stall large.

Maybe after each phase, if best_score unchanged and current drifted, reset.

Let's design `optimize_sequence` code more cleanly:
```
def optimize_sequence(start, time_budget, rng):
    a = normalize_array(start)
    if a is None or a.size < 2:
       a = np.ones(1024)/1024
    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
    cur_score = score_from_conv(a,c)
    if not finite: cur_score=2.0
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    total = max(2.0, float(time_budget))
    t0 = time.monotonic(); end = t0 + total
    phases = ((5.0,0.15),(10.0,0.20),(20.0,0.25),(40.0,0.25),(80.0,0.15))
    phase_start=t0
    for beta, frac in phases:
       phase_end = min(end, phase_start + frac*total)
       phase_start = phase_end
       if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 0.05: continue
       eta = 0.25 / np.sqrt(max(1.0, beta/10.0))
       F = smooth_max_value(c,beta)
       stall=0
       while time.monotonic() < phase_end - 0.05:
          if cur_score > best_score + 0.01:
             a=best_a.copy(); c=self_convolution_fft(a); cur_score=best_score; F=smooth_max_value(c,beta); eta=0.05
          if not np.isfinite(cur_score): ...
          w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
          G=correlation_aw_fft(a,w)
          if G.size != a.size: G=np.zeros(a.size)
          u=G - np.mean(G)
          scale=float(np.max(np.abs(u))) if u.size else 0.0
          accepted=False
          if scale > 1e-14:
             # exp current
             cand=exponentiated_candidate(a,u,scale,eta)
             if cand is not None:
                c2=self_convolution_fft(cand); sc2,F2=candidate_metrics(cand,c2,beta)
                if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    accept best
                elif sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and F2 < F - 1e-18:
                    accept current
             # if not accepted, try half
             if not accepted: param=eta*0.5; ...
             if not accepted: param=eta*0.25; ...
             if not accepted:
                cand=ratio_candidate(a,G,1.0)
                if cand is not None: ...
             if not accepted:
                cand=projected_candidate(a,u,scale,eta)
                if ...
          if not accepted:
             stall +=1
             eta = max(0.02, eta*0.65)
             if stall >= 5:
                # random
                sigma = 0.02 + 0.02*(stall % 7)
                cand = block_perturb(a,rng,sigma)
                if cand is not None:
                   c2=...; sc2,F2=...
                   if np.isfinite(sc2):
                      if sc2 < best_score:
                         best_score=sc2; best_a=cand.copy(); a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=F2; accepted=True
                      elif sc2 <= best_score + 0.002 and stall > 20:
                         a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=F2
                if stall > 60:
                    a=best_a.copy(); c=self_convolution_fft(a); cur_score=best_score; F=smooth_max_value(c,beta); eta=0.05; stall=10
          # small time guard
    return best_a, best_score
```

`exact_score_of_sequence`:
```
def exact_score_of_sequence(seq):
    try:
       lst = sequence_to_list(seq)
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
    try:
       val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
    try:
       val = float(val)
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
    return val
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    reserve = min(120.0, max(60.0, budget*0.06))
    search_deadline = t_start + max(30.0, budget - reserve)
    best_exact_seq = None
    best_exact_score = np.inf

    glob = get_initial_global()
    if glob is not None:
       if glob.size > _MAX_OPT_N: glob = resample_sequence(glob, _MAX_OPT_N)
       glob = normalize_array(glob)
    fallback = None
    if glob is not None:
       fallback = glob.copy()
       es = exact_score_of_sequence(glob)
       if np.isfinite(es): best_exact_score=es; best_exact_seq=glob.copy()
    if fallback is None:
       fallback = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)/1024.0
       es=exact_score_of_sequence(fallback)
       if finite... (maybe skip to save? fallback score 2)
```

Pseudo code final for optimize:
```
def optimize_sequence(start, time_budget, rng):
    a = normalize_array(start)
    if a is None or a.size < 2:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
    cur_score = score_from_conv(a, c)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        cur_score = 2.0
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    total = max(2.0, float(time_budget))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + total
    phases = ((5.0, 0.15), (10.0, 0.20), (20.0, 0.25), (40.0, 0.25), (80.0, 0.15))
    # deadlines
    cumulative=0.0
    phase_deadlines=[]
    for beta, frac in phases:
       cumulative += frac
       phase_deadlines.append((beta, min(t_end, t0 + cumulative*total)))
    for beta, phase_end in phase_deadlines:
       if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 0.05:
          continue
       eta = 0.25 / np.sqrt(max(1.0, beta / 10.0))
       F = smooth_max_value(c, beta)
       stall = 0
       while time.monotonic() < phase_end - 0.05:
          if (not np.isfinite(cur_score)) or (best_score - cur_score > 0.01? Actually if cur > best+0.01):
             ...
          if not np.isfinite(F): F=smooth_max_value(c,beta)
          w = softmax_weights(c, beta)
          G = correlation_aw_fft(a, w)
          if G.size != a.size:
              G = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
          u = G - np.mean(G)
          scale = float(np.max(np.abs(u))) if u.size else 0.0
          accepted = False
          if scale > 1e-14:
              # exp etas
              for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                  param = eta * mult
                  cand = exponentiated_candidate(a, u, scale, param)
                  if cand is None: continue
                  c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
                  sc2, F2 = candidate_metrics(cand, c2, beta)
                  if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                      best_score = sc2; best_a = cand.copy(); a = cand; c = c2; cur_score = sc2; F = F2
                      eta = max(0.02, min(1.0, param * 1.2)); stall=0; accepted=True; break
                  if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and F2 + 1e-15 < F:
                      a = cand; c = c2; cur_score = sc2; F = F2
                      eta = max(0.02, min(1.0, param)); stall=0; accepted=True; break
              if not accepted:
                  cand = ratio_candidate(a, G, 1.0)
                  if cand is not None:
                      c2 = ...; sc2,F2=...
                      if sc2 < best... update best accepted
                      elif sc2 <= best+1e-8 and F2+1e-15<F: accept current
              if not accepted:
                  cand = projected_candidate(a, u, scale, eta)
                  if cand is not None: ...
          if not accepted:
             stall += 1
             eta = max(0.015, eta * 0.65)
             if stall >= 4:
                sigma = 0.02 + 0.01 * float(stall % 8)
                cand = block_perturb(a, rng, sigma)
                if cand is not None:
                   c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
                   sc2, F2 = candidate_metrics(cand, c2, beta)
                   if np.isfinite(sc2):
                      if sc2 < best_score:
                         best_score=sc2; best_a=cand.copy(); a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=F2; accepted=True
                      elif sc2 <= best_score + 0.003 and stall > 15:
                         a=cand; c=c2; cur_score=sc2; F=F2
             if stall > 80:
                a = best_a.copy(); c = self_convolution_fft(a); cur_score = best_score; F = smooth_max_value(c,beta); eta=0.05; stall=10
    return best_a, best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
using shape-prior generation (square-root decay families), FFT-based
smooth-max gradient steps on the simplex, multiplicative/ratio updates,
and light random local perturbations.  The best candidate by exact
evaluate_sequence() is returned.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

_MAX_OPT_N = 12000


def normalize_array(a):
    """Return a non-negative 1D float array with sum 1, or None."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    arr = arr / s
    return arr


def resample_sequence(arr, target):
    """Contiguous average downsample to target length."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    target = int(target)
    if target <= 1:
        return normalize_array(arr)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if arr.size <= target:
        return normalize_array(arr)
    out = np.zeros(target, dtype=np.float64)
    n = arr.size
    for i in range(target):
        a = int(i * n / target)
        b = int((i + 1) * n / target)
        if b <= a:
            b = a + 1
        out[i] = float(np.mean(arr[a:b]))
    return normalize_array(out)


def get_initial_global():
    """Fetch the global height_sequence_1 if available and valid."""
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return arr / s


def self_convolution_fft(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a, using FFT for larger n."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a, mode="full"), dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < need:
        nfft <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=nfft)
    return np.asarray(c[:need], dtype=np.float64)


def correlation_aw_fft(a, w):
    """
    Return G[j] = sum_i a[i] * w[i+j], j=0..n-1,
    where len(a)=n and len(w)=2n-1 in the usual fast path.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m < n:
        m = n
        w = np.pad(w, (0, m - w.size))
    if n <= 256:
        try:
            g = np.correlate(w, a, mode="valid")
            return np.asarray(g[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            pass
    need = n + m - 1
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < need:
        nfft <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    wr = np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, n=nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=nfft)
    L = m - 1
    start = n - 1
    stop = L + 1
    if start < 0:
        start = 0
    if stop > conv.size:
        stop = conv.size
    if start >= stop:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    seg = conv[start:stop]
    g = seg[::-1]
    if g.size != n:
        g = np.resize(g, n)
    return np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)


def score_from_conv(a, c):
    """Score from already computed convolution c."""
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def score_from_array(a):
    """Score using FFT convolution."""
    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
    return score_from_conv(a, c)


def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    """
    Weights proportional to exp(beta*(c/cmax - 1)).
    beta controls sharpening toward the maximum lags.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(c)
    r = c / cm
    r = np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)
    z = float(beta) * (r - 1.0)
    z = np.where(z < -700.0, -700.0, z)
    z = np.where(z > 0.0, 0.0, z)
    w = np.exp(z)
    if float(np.max(w)) <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(c)
    return w


def smooth_max_value(c, beta):
    """
    Smooth surrogate:
        cmax * (1 + (1/beta) log sum exp(beta*(c/cmax - 1)))
    It is close to cmax when a few lags dominate, and shrinks when the
    peak is reduced or the active peak set is smoothed out.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0
    r = c / cm
    r = np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)
    z = float(beta) * (r - 1.0)
    z = np.where(z < -700.0, -700.0, z)
    z = np.where(z > 0.0, 0.0, z)
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return cm
    return float(cm * (1.0 + np.log(s) / float(beta)))


def candidate_metrics(cand, c, beta):
    """Return exact score and smooth surrogate for a candidate."""
    sc = score_from_conv(cand, c)
    f = smooth_max_value(c, beta)
    return sc, f


def exponentiated_candidate(a, u, scale, eta):
    """Mirror-step candidate: a * exp(-eta*u/scale), normalized."""
    if scale <= 1e-300:
        return None
    z = -float(eta) * (u / scale)
    z = np.where(z < -20.0, -20.0, z)
    z = np.where(z > 20.0, 20.0, z)
    cand = a * np.exp(z)
    return normalize_array(cand)


def ratio_candidate(a, G, alpha=1.0):
    """KKT-like multiplicative update a / (G + alpha*mean(G))."""
    G = np.asarray(G, dtype=np.float64)
    Gp = np.maximum(G, 0.0)
    gm = float(np.mean(Gp))
    if gm <= 1e-300:
        return None
    denom = Gp + float(alpha) * gm + 1e-300
    new = a / denom
    s = float(np.sum(new))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    new = new / s
    cand = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * new
    return normalize_array(cand)


def project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto the probability simplex."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    inds = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * inds > (cssv - 1.0))[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        w = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        r = int(rho[-1])
        theta = (cssv[r] - 1.0) / (r + 1.0)
        w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def projected_candidate(a, u, scale, eta):
    """Projected-gradient-style candidate."""
    if scale <= 1e-300:
        return None
    v = a - float(eta) * (u / scale)
    return project_simplex(v)


def random_noise_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    """Multiplicative random noise perturbation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), a.size)
    cand = a * np.exp(noise)
    return normalize_array(cand)


def block_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    """Random block multiplicative perturbation; occasionally symmetrize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return random_noise_perturb(a, rng, sigma)
    minw = max(1, n // 500)
    maxw = n - 1
    if maxw < minw:
        maxw = minw
    tmp = maxw // 10
    if tmp > minw:
        minw = max(1, minw)
        maxw = max(minw + 1, tmp)
        if maxw > n - 1:
            maxw = n - 1
    width = int(rng.integers(minw, maxw + 1))
    if width <= 0 or width >= n:
        width = max(1, min(n // 20, n - 1))
    start_high = n - width
    if start_high <= 0:
        start = 0
    else:
        start = int(rng.integers(0, start_high + 1))
    cand = a.copy()
    factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma))))
    cand[start:start + width] *= factor
    if rng.random() < 0.20:
        cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
    return normalize_array(cand)


def central_binomial_shape(n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1 - x)^(-1/2), truncated.
    This is a strong prior: its square is very flat on the first half.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return normalize_array(a)


def power_alpha_shape(n, alpha):
    """Decreasing power law prior."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(i, -float(alpha))
    return normalize_array(a)


def generate_candidates(n, rng):
    """Generate a diverse set of normalized non-negative starts."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return [normalize_array(np.ones(2, dtype=np.float64))]
    out = []
    b = central_binomial_shape(n)
    if b is None:
        b = power_alpha_shape(n, 0.5)
    if b is None:
        return []
    out.append(b)

    pos = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ps = pos / float(n - 1) if n > 1 else np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Tail-boosted square-root shapes.
    for q in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        for gamma in (0.25, 0.50, 1.00, 1.50):
            a = b * (1.0 + gamma * (ps ** q))
            arr = normalize_array(a)
            if arr is not None:
                out.append(arr)

    # Small reverse blends.
    rev = b[::-1]
    for mix in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20):
        a = (1.0 - mix) * b + mix * rev
        arr = normalize_array(a)
        if arr is not None:
            out.append(arr)

    # Pure power laws near the optimal asymptotic exponent 1/2.
    for alpha in (0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.65):
        arr = power_alpha_shape(n, alpha)
        if arr is not None:
            out.append(arr)

    # Offset power laws.
    for r in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 1.00):
        a = np.power(pos + 1.0 + r * float(n), -0.5)
        arr = normalize_array(a)
        if arr is not None:
            out.append(arr)

    # Low-frequency modulations of the square-root prior.
    for freq in (1, 2, 3):
        for amp in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30):
            factor = 1.0 - amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * ps)
            factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.05)
            arr = normalize_array(b * factor)
            if arr is not None:
                out.append(arr)

    # Random smooth starts.
    for sigma in (0.20, 0.50):
        a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
        arr = normalize_array(a)
        if arr is not None:
            out.append(arr)

    cleaned = []
    for arr in out:
        arr = normalize_array(arr)
        if arr is not None and arr.size == n:
            cleaned.append(arr)
    return cleaned


def optimize_sequence(start, time_budget, rng):
    """
    Improve one start sequence under a time budget.
    Uses beta-annealed smooth-max surrogate, FFT gradient, exponentiated
    mirror steps, ratio fixed-point steps, projected steps, and random
    perturbations.
    """
    a = normalize_array(start)
    if a is None or a.size < 2:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
    cur_score = score_from_conv(a, c)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        cur_score = 2.0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(cur_score)

    total = max(2.0, float(time_budget))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + total

    # beta works on ratios c/cmax, so these are meaningful sharpenings.
    phases = (
        (3.0, 0.15),
        (6.0, 0.20),
        (12.0, 0.25),
        (24.0, 0.25),
        (48.0, 0.15),
    )
    cumulative = 0.0
    phase_deadlines = []
    for beta, frac in phases:
        cumulative += float(frac)
        phase_deadlines.append((float(beta), min(t_end, t0 + cumulative * total)))

    for beta, phase_end in phase_deadlines:
        if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 0.05:
            continue

        eta = 0.25 / np.sqrt(max(1.0, beta / 10.0))
        F = smooth_max_value(c, beta)
        stall = 0

        while time.monotonic() < phase_end - 0.05:
            if (not np.isfinite(cur_score)) or (cur_score > best_score + 0.01):
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = self_convolution_fft(a)
                cur_score = best_score
                F = smooth_max_value(c, beta)
                eta = 0.05

            w = softmax_weights(c, beta)
            G = correlation_aw_fft(a, w)
            if G.size != a.size:
                G = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)

            u = G - float(np.mean(G))
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(u))) if u.size else 0.0
            accepted = False

            if scale > 1e-14:
                # Exponentiated mirror steps with backtracking.
                for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                    param = eta * mult
                    cand = exponentiated_candidate(a, u, scale, param)
                    if cand is None:
                        continue
                    c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
                    sc2, F2 = candidate_metrics(cand, c2, beta)
                    if not np.isfinite(sc2):
                        continue

                    if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = float(sc2)
                        best_a = cand.copy()
                        a = cand
                        c = c2
                        cur_score = float(sc2)
                        F = float(F2)
                        eta = max(0.02, min(1.0, float(param) * 1.2))
                        stall = 0
                        accepted = True
                        break

                    if sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and F2 + 1e-12 < F:
                        a = cand
                        c = c2
                        cur_score = float(sc2)
                        F = float(F2)
                        eta = max(0.02, min(1.0, float(param)))
                        stall = 0
                        accepted = True
                        break

                # More aggressive KKT-like ratio update.
                if not accepted:
                    cand = ratio_candidate(a, G, 1.0)
                    if cand is not None:
                        c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
                        sc2, F2 = candidate_metrics(cand, c2, beta)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2):
                            if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                                best_score = float(sc2)
                                best_a = cand.copy()
                                a = cand
                                c = c2
                                cur_score = float(sc2)
                                F = float(F2)
                                stall = 0
                                accepted = True
                            elif sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and F2 + 1e-12 < F:
                                a = cand
                                c = c2
                                cur_score = float(sc2)
                                F = float(F2)
                                stall = 0
                                accepted = True

                # Projected Euclidean step as a different geometry.
                if not accepted:
                    cand = projected_candidate(a, u, scale, eta)
                    if cand is not None:
                        c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
                        sc2, F2 = candidate_metrics(cand, c2, beta)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2):
                            if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                                best_score = float(sc2)
                                best_a = cand.copy()
                                a = cand
                                c = c2
                                cur_score = float(sc2)
                                F = float(F2)
                                eta = max(0.02, min(1.0, float(eta) * 1.1))
                                stall = 0
                                accepted = True
                            elif sc2 <= best_score + 1e-8 and F2 + 1e-12 < F:
                                a = cand
                                c = c2
                                cur_score = float(sc2)
                                F = float(F2)
                                stall = 0
                                accepted = True

            if not accepted:
                stall += 1
                eta = max(0.015, eta * 0.65)

                if stall >= 4:
                    sigma = 0.02 + 0.01 * float(stall % 8)
                    cand = block_perturb(a, rng, sigma)
                    if cand is None:
                        cand = random_noise_perturb(a, rng, sigma)
                    if cand is not None:
                        c2 = self_convolution_fft(cand)
                        sc2, F2 = candidate_metrics(cand, c2, beta)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2):
                            if sc2 < best_score:
                                best_score = float(sc2)
                                best_a = cand.copy()
                                a = cand
                                c = c2
                                cur_score = float(sc2)
                                F = float(F2)
                                accepted = True
                            elif sc2 <= best_score + 0.003 and stall > 15:
                                a = cand
                                c = c2
                                cur_score = float(sc2)
                                F = float(F2)

                if stall > 80:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    c = self_convolution_fft(a)
                    cur_score = best_score
                    F = smooth_max_value(c, beta)
                    eta = 0.05
                    stall = 10

    return best_a, best_score


def sequence_to_list(seq):
    """Convert to a plain list of Python floats."""
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = []
    for x in arr:
        out.append(float(x))
    return out


def exact_score_of_sequence(seq):
    """Call the visible evaluator exactly; return inf on failure."""
    try:
        lst = sequence_to_list(seq)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Main search: generate strong starts, optimize several, exact-polish."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Reserve time for final exact evaluations and return safety.
    reserve = min(150.0, max(60.0, 0.07 * budget))
    search_deadline = t_start + max(30.0, budget - reserve)

    best_exact_seq = None
    best_exact_score = np.inf

    glob = get_initial_global()
    if glob is not None:
        if glob.size > _MAX_OPT_N:
            glob = resample_sequence(glob, _MAX_OPT_N)
        glob = normalize_array(glob)

    fallback = None
    if glob is not None:
        fallback = glob.copy()
        es = exact_score_of_sequence(glob)
        if np.isfinite(es):
            best_exact_score = es
            best_exact_seq = glob.copy()

    if fallback is None:
        fallback = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        es = exact_score_of_sequence(fallback)
        if np.isfinite(es):
            best_exact_score = es
            best_exact_seq = fallback.copy()

    # Choose working length for generated starts.
    n_main = 10000
    if glob is not None:
        L = int(glob.size)
        if 4096 <= L <= 10000:
            n_main = L

    raw_candidates = []
    if glob is not None:
        raw_candidates.append((0, glob))
    gen = generate_candidates(n_main, rng)
    for arr in gen:
        raw_candidates.append((1, arr))

    scored = []
    for idx, (pr, arr) in enumerate(raw_candidates):
        arr = normalize_array(arr)
        if arr is None or arr.size < 2 or arr.size > _MAX_OPT_N:
            continue
        sc = score_from_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), int(pr), int(idx), arr))

    if not scored:
        return sequence_to_list(fallback)

    scored.sort()

    global_item = None
    for item in scored:
        if item[1] == 0:
            global_item = item
            break

    final_items = []
    if global_item is not None:
        final_items.append(global_item)
        non_cap = 4
    else:
        non_cap = 5

    non_count = 0
    for item in scored:
        if item[1] == 0:
            continue
        if non_count < non_cap:
            final_items.append(item)
            non_count += 1

    L = len(final_items)
    if L == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif L == 2:
        fracs = [0.60, 0.40]
    elif L == 3:
        fracs = [0.50, 0.30, 0.20]
    elif L == 4:
        fracs = [0.45, 0.30, 0.15, 0.10]
    elif L == 5:
        fracs = [0.40, 0.27, 0.18, 0.10, 0.05]
    else:
        fracs = [0.30, 0.25, 0.17, 0.12, 0.10, 0.06][:L]

    final_candidates = []
    counter = 0

    for idx, item in enumerate(final_items):
        if time.monotonic() >= search_deadline - 10.0:
            break
        sc0, pr, unused, arr = item
        frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else fracs[-1]
        t_allow = max(20.0, frac * max(1.0, search_deadline - t_start))
        remaining = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
        t_allow = min(t_allow, max(10.0, remaining - 5.0))
        if t_allow <= 5.0:
            break

        try:
            best_arr, best_sc = optimize_sequence(arr, t_allow, rng)
        except Exception:
            best_arr, best_sc = arr, sc0

        best_arr = normalize_array(best_arr)
        if best_arr is None:
            continue
        try:
            bsc = score_from_array(best_arr)
        except Exception:
            bsc = best_sc
        if not np.isfinite(bsc):
            bsc = 1e9
        final_candidates.append((float(bsc), counter, best_arr))
        counter += 1

    # One final polish on the best FFT candidate if time remains.
    if final_candidates and time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 25.0:
        polys = [(float(sc), i, arr) for i, (sc, arr) in enumerate(final_candidates)]
        polys.sort()
        rem = search_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
        if rem > 30.0 and polys:
            try:
                best_arr, best_sc = optimize_sequence(polys[0][2], rem, rng)
                best_arr = normalize_array(best_arr)
                if best_arr is not None:
                    try:
                        bsc = score_from_array(best_arr)
                    except Exception:
                        bsc = best_sc
                    if not np.isfinite(bsc):
                        bsc = best_sc
                    final_candidates.append((float(bsc), counter, best_arr))
                    counter += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Exact final evaluation of the most promising FFT candidates.
    candidates = []
    for i, (sc, idx, arr) in enumerate(final_candidates):
        candidates.append((float(sc), i, arr))
    if fallback is not None:
        try:
            fs = score_from_array(fallback)
        except Exception:
            fs = 2.0
        if not np.isfinite(fs):
            fs = 2.0
        candidates.append((float(fs), 10**6, fallback))
    candidates.sort()

    evaluated = 0
    hard_deadline = t_start + budget - 3.0
    for sc, i, arr in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline:
            break
        if evaluated >= 8:
            break
        arr = normalize_array(arr)
        if arr is None:
            continue
        es = exact_score_of_sequence(arr)
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = es
            best_exact_seq = arr.copy()
        evaluated += 1

    if best_exact_seq is None:
        if candidates:
            best_exact_seq = candidates[0][2].copy()
        else:
            best_exact_seq = fallback.copy()

    best_exact_seq = normalize_array(best_exact_seq)
    if best_exact_seq is None:
        best_exact_seq = fallback.copy()

    return sequence_to_list(best_exact_seq)
```
